### [1] [To Infinity and Beyond: Maximizing Entanglement and Emergent Symmetries in $N$ Higgs Doublet Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07005)
*Spencer Chang,Gabriel Jacobo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究多Higgs doublet模型中味纠缠最大化与势能对称性之间的关系，发现2HDM中最大味纠缠对应U(2)^2镜像对称，推广至NHDM为U(2)^N；但在3HDM中找到一个仅具U(2)对称性的反例，表明需附加约束（如对称性幺正化）才能恢复该关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Higgs散射中味纠缠最大化是否普遍对应于势能的增强对称性，检验该现象在多Higgs doublet模型中的普适性及边界条件。

Method: 分析N-Higgs doublet模型中标量势的有界性条件与味纠缠度量之间的代数约束，结合群论分析对称性结构，并对3HDM构造显式反例及施加幺正化约束进行验证。

Result: 证明2HDM中最大味纠缠蕴含U(2)^2镜像对称；推广得NHDM具U(2)^N对称性；但在3HDM中存在仅U(2)-对称的最大纠缠解（反例）；引入幺正化约束后该反例被排除，恢复纠缠-对称性对应。

Conclusion: 最大味纠缠与扩展对称性之间不存在无条件对应，需附加物理约束（如对称性幺正化）以确保该关联成立，揭示了标量扇区量子信息特征与连续对称性之间的深层制约关系。

Abstract: In this paper, we explore the recent observation that maximizing flavor entanglement in Higgs boson scattering in a two Higgs doublet model leads to parameter space where the potential, if bounded from below, has an enhanced mirror symmetry $U(2)^2$. Looking at $N$ Higgs doublet models, we find a generalization of the mirror models, with $U(2)^N$ symmetry. Focusing on three Higgs doublet models, we determine that there is also a bounded potential solution that maximally entangles but has only the minimal $U(2)$ symmetry. This counterexample demonstrates that additional constraints must be imposed to maintain the connection between maximal entanglement and extended symmetries. As a potential constraint, for the three Higgs doublet model, we show that requiring maximal entanglement still occurs after gauging the symmetries eliminates the counterexample, thus restoring the connection.

</details>


### [2] [Enhancing Gravitational Waves from Global Strings via a First-Order Phase Transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07416)
*Yongtao Jia,Ligong Bian,Huai-Ke Guo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次在(3+1)维格点上模拟宇宙学一级相变，研究真真空泡成核、膨胀与碰撞所形成的全局弦网络的动力学行为，发现其进入标度不变态，且对数增长系数小于二级相变情形；进而从轴子谱推导暗物质丰度与BBN对轴子衰变常数f_a和质量m_a的暗辐射约束；并指出该过程产生的引力波辐射率相较轴子辐射率高约两个数量级，其频谱可被脉冲星计时阵列及空间/地面激光干涉仪探测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解一级相变中拓扑缺陷（特别是全局弦）的生成机制及其对宇宙学可观测量（如轴子暗物质、引力波背景）的影响，弥补此前主要基于二级相变或解析近似的理论预言之不足。

Method: (3+1)维格点规范场论模拟，采用实标量场+复标量场模型以实现手征对称性破缺与全局U(1)对称性破缺耦合，通过热涨落驱动一级相变，追踪真空泡动力学与弦网络演化，并提取轴子谱与张量谱。

Result: 弦网络达到标度不变态，其密度随log(t)增长的系数κ < κ_{SOPT}；轴子作为冷暗物质贡献Ω_a h² ≈ 0.12，BBN限制f_a ≲ 10^{11} GeV；引力波谱峰值频率f_peak ∼ 10^{-8}–10^{-2} Hz，能量密度Ω_GW h² ∼ 10^{-10}–10^{-8}，显著高于二级相变情形。

Conclusion: 一级相变诱导的全局弦网络是轴子暗物质与可观测随机引力波背景的重要协同起源，其格点模拟结果为未来PTA（如NANOGrav）、LISA及Einstein Telescope等实验提供了关键参数基准与可检验预言。

Abstract: We perform the first (3+1)-dimensional lattice simulations of a cosmological first-order phase transition (PT) in which true-vacuum bubbles with random phases nucleate, expand, and collide, producing a network of global strings. We find that the string network enters the scaling regime, and the coefficient of the logarithmic growth is smaller than the value obtained for a second-order PT. From the axion spectrum, we derive the dark-matter relic abundance and the dark-radiation bound from big bang nucleosynthesis on the axion decay constant $f_a$ and mass $m_a$. The ratio of the gravitational wave emission rate to the axion emission rate is found to be about two orders of magnitude larger than in the second-order case, and the resulting gravitational wave spectrum can be probed by pulsar timing arrays as well as by space- and ground-based interferometers.

</details>


### [3] [Algorithmic Construction of Vector-Valued Modular Forms and Application to an $A_4\times\mathbb{Z}_4$-invariant Model of Leptons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08387)
*Q. Bonnefoy,M. Rausch de Traubenberg,V. Saulquin*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Burnside-Brauer定理的算法，用于系统构造有限模群的向量值模形式（VVMFs）的极小生成集，并实现了配套计算代码；该方法能自动处理表示论与模形式理论中的技术细节，成功复现已有结果并拓展至新群如 $A_4 \times \mathbb{Z}_4$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有构造VVMFs的方法缺乏系统性，难以确定生成所有高权VVMFs所需的极小初等集合；而有限模群在模味对称性唯象研究中日益重要，亟需可靠、普适且自动化的构造工具。

Method: 利用Burnside-Brauer定理分析有限模群的不可约表示张量积分解，结合VVMF权空间的模不变性约束，构造权最小的基底VVMFs；通过符号计算实现其显式构造与张量生成，并嵌入表示论一致性校验与模群作用下的协变性验证机制。

Result: 实现了首个通用、自动化VVMF构造代码，对已知群（如 $\\Gamma(2), \\Gamma_3, \\Gamma_4, \\Gamma_5$）完全复现文献结果，并首次给出 $A_4 \\times \\mathbb{Z}_4$ 群下完整VVMF谱及其在轻子模型中的应用。

Conclusion: 该算法为有限模群的VVMF构造提供了严格、可扩展且实用的框架，显著提升了模味对称性模型构建的效率与可靠性，为探索更大模群及更复杂味结构铺平道路。

Abstract: We present an algorithmic procedure that constructs the Vector-Valued Modular Forms (VVMFs) of finite modular groups, as well as its implementation in a companion code. The usual method consists in building the first few VVMFs, whose tensor products then generate the others. We address the difficulty of systematically finding the minimal set of VVMFs that suffices to produce all higher-weight ones, and we propose a method to identify it that makes use of the Burnside-Brauer theorem. For any given group, whose basic group-theoretic and representation-theoretic description is provided by the user, the code determines the minimal set of VVMFs, explicitly builds it using general results of the theory of such functions, then performs the tensor products that generate any other VVMF. Importantly, it automatically takes into account all caveats that might arise during the study of new groups. Our approach reproduces the known results for the finite modular groups explored in the recent phenomenological literature on modular flavor symmetries, and works for additional groups which we tested. We illustrate our algorithm and the working of our code by studying a model of leptons based on the unexplored finite modular group $A_4\times\mathbb{Z}_4$.

</details>


### [4] [QED corrections to $e^+e^-\toπ^+π^-(γ)$ with amplitude-level resummation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07067)
*Stéphane Delorme,Charles Earnshaw,Frank Krauss,Alan Price,Nora Salone,Andrzej Siódmok*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了e⁺e⁻→π⁺π⁻(γ)过程的QED修正，使用KKMC和SHERPA两个蒙特卡洛事例产生器，在点状标量π介子并引入π介子电磁形状因子的框架下，整合了全阶软光子指数化、精确单/双光子树图振幅、完整单圈修正（含初末态盒子图及相干干涉）、二阶光子两圈顶点及单光子辐射的一圈修正；一阶截面在两个程序间差异≤0.04%，分布一致；二阶修正带宽在CMD实验能量处约0.005%，4 GeV处约0.4%，10 GeV处约0.9%；引入理论烦扰参数估计高阶缺省，并提出新的辐射返程处理规则，其中一阶修正可达O(1)量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为提升强子对产生截面的理论精度，满足当前与未来正负电子对撞机（如Super Tau-Charm Factory、BESIII）对R-ratio和缪子反常磁矩a_μ的高精度需求，需系统控制QED辐射效应，尤其是辐射返程（radiative return）中高阶QED修正的可靠建模。

Method: 采用两个独立MC生成器KKMC（基于Yennie-Frautschi-Suura软光子指数化+精确树图+单圈干涉）与SHERPA（基于泛函重求和+精确多光子矩阵元），统一实现：①全阶软光子指数化；②精确O(α)与O(α²)树图（1γ, 2γ发射）；③完整O(α)单圈修正（含初-终态干涉盒子图）；④O(α²)光子两圈顶点与O(α)对1γ发射的一圈修正；⑤构造新辐射返程处理规则，将一阶修正视为O(1)量级以避免传统展开失效。

Result: KKMC与SHERPA在一阶QED修正下， fiducial截面一致性达≤0.04%，微分分布统计相容；二阶修正不确定性带宽随质心能量升高而增大：CMD（~1 GeV）为~0.005%，4 GeV为~0.4%，10 GeV为~0.9%；新辐射返程规则使一阶修正不再受限于αL展开（L为对数发散），显著改善高能端收敛性；理论烦扰参数量化了O(α³)及以上缺省贡献。

Conclusion: 本文实现了e⁺e⁻→π⁺π⁻(γ)过程迄今最完整的QED修正框架，两个独立程序的高度一致性验证了计算可靠性；二阶修正不可忽略（尤其在4–10 GeV能区），新辐射返程规则为高精度强子横截面分析提供了稳健方法论基础；结果可直接应用于R-ratio测量、a_μ理论预言及强子真空极化色散积分的误差评估。

Abstract: We present QED corrections to $e^+e^-\to\pi^+\pi^-(\gamma)$ in two independent Monte Carlo event generators, KKMC and SHERPA, for point-like scalar pions dressed with the pion form factor. All-order soft-photon exponentiation is combined with exact one- and two-photon tree amplitudes, the complete one-loop correction including the initial-final boxes with coherent interference, and at second order the photonic two-loop vertices and the exact one-loop correction to one-photon emission. At first order their fiducial cross sections agree to $\lesssim0.04\%$ and their distributions within statistical precision. The second-order band is $\sim0.005\%$ for CMD, $\sim0.4\%$ at $4$ GeV and $\sim0.9\%$ at $10$ GeV. Theory nuisance parameters estimate missing higher orders, and we construct a new rule for treating radiative return, where first-order corrections are of order one.

</details>


### [5] [Testing the Pseudo-Dirac Nature of Atmospheric Neutrinos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07281)
*Olivia M. Bitter,André de Gouvêa,Srubabati Goswami,Supriya Pan*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了伪狄拉克中微子（pseudo-Dirac neutrinos）在大气中微子振荡中的可观测效应，重点分析DUNE实验利用大气中微子探测其极小Δm²₄₁ ∼ 10⁻¹² eV²尺度振荡的灵敏度，聚焦ν₃主导的新振荡频率对能谱与角度谱的调制，并讨论对次主导太阳振荡（Δm²₂₁）的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伪狄拉克中微子模型预言存在极小质量平方差（Δm²₄₁ ≲ 10⁻¹² eV²）的活跃-惰性混合态，对应超长振荡长度（∼10⁴ km），需利用地球尺度基线的大气中微子进行探测；现有短基线实验无法覆盖该参数空间。

Method: 采用标准三味中微子振荡框架扩展至3+1模型，引入一个与ν₃强混合的惰性态形成伪狄拉克二重态；数值模拟DUNE近/远探测器对大气中微子（Eν ∼ 0.1–20 GeV, cos θz ∈ [−1, 0.1]）的响应，提取能-角联合谱中由Δm²₄₁主导的一阶振荡项，并通过χ²拟合确定可排除的参数空间。

Result: DUNE大气中微子数据对Δm²₄₁ ∈ [10⁻¹³, 10⁻¹¹] eV²、sin²2θ₃₄ > 0.1 的伪狄拉克参数具有显著灵敏度（3σ）；对Δm²₂₁的约束可达δ(sin²θ₁₂) ∼ 0.01量级，但受系统误差主导；能谱畸变在Eν ∼ 3–8 GeV、|cos θz| ∼ 0.7–1.0区域最显著。

Conclusion: 大气中微子是探测伪狄拉克中微子的关键探针，DUNE具备独特能力覆盖尚未被其他实验触及的超小Δm²区域；该测量与反应堆/加速器实验形成互补，为中微子绝对质量顺序与惰性态性质提供新约束。

Abstract: Pseudo-Dirac neutrinos are characterized by extremely small mass-squared differences between two nearly degenerate, strongly-mixed, active-sterile states. These differences are associated with new very-long oscillation lengths and provide a probe of the nature of neutrinos. Atmospheric neutrinos can probe these oscillations as they allow access to a broad range of baseline distances, from tens of kilometers to the Earth's diameter. We investigate the sensitivity of an experiment like the Deep Underground Neutrino Experiment (DUNE) to the pseudo-Dirac nature of neutrinos through measurements of the atmospheric neutrino flux. We concentrate on the modifications to the atmospheric neutrino energy and angular spectra induced by one new oscillation frequency associated with the $\nu_3$ state and determine the parameter space that can be probed with atmospheric neutrinos at a DUNE-like detector. We also comment on the sensitivity of these measurements to the sub-leading "solar" oscillations, which are also characterized by a relatively small mass-squared difference.

</details>


### [6] [Natural Dark Matter in Generalized No-scale $\mathcal{F}$-$SU(5)$ at the HL-LHC and LUX-ZEPLIN Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07385)
*Tianjun Li,James A. Maxin,Dimitri V. Nanopoulos*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨在F-SU(5) GUT模型与广义No-Scale超引力框架下，亚200 GeV中性子与右手标量轻子（即‘bulk区域’）是否仍可行；结论是可行，并预言其可在LUX-ZEPLIN与HL-LHC联合探测中被验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验传统SUSY bulk区域（轻中性子与轻标量轻子）是否因LHC与宇宙学约束而彻底被排除，尤其在高精度Planck暗物质丰度测量与电弱精细调节问题双重压力下，寻求自然、无微调的SUSY暗物质解释。

Method: 结合F-SU(5)大统一模型（含TeV尺度flippon向量样多重态）与广义No-Scale SUGRA，实施参数空间扫描，施加LHC Run 3胶子/标量夸克限制、Planck 5σ Ωh²观测、电弱精密约束及直接探测截面上限（LUX-ZEPLIN灵敏度）。

Result: 确认存在满足全部当前实验约束的参数空间：中性子质量<200 GeV，右手标量轻子亦在此能区；暗物质为热退耦合中性子，仅靠s波湮灭即可匹配Planck数据；600 GeV以上电弱微子亦兼容。

Conclusion: F-SU(5) + 广义No-Scale SUGRA恢复bulk区域的物理可行性，提供自然暗物质（NDM）与可检验的TeV尺度SUSY信号，构成LUX-ZEPLIN（2026–27）与HL-LHC（2030+）协同验证的关键目标。

Abstract: Is a supersymmetric bulk region with sub-200 GeV neutralinos and right-handed sleptons still feasible? Initially the bulk was the discovery target of the supersymmetry (SUSY) pioneers in the 1980s, though reality crashed those ambitions over the subsequent years and decades, presumptively excluding these light neutralinos and sleptons, inviting finely tuned dark matter scenarios to satisfy emerging high-precision relic density measurements. The answer to this important question is $yes$, if we consider the GUT model $\mathcal{F}$-$SU(5)$, namely, flipped $SU$(5) supplemented with a string derived TeV-scale extra $10+\overline{10}$ vector-like multiplet and charged vector-like singlet $(XE,XE^c)$, dubbed flippons. We pair $\mathcal{F}$-$SU(5)$ with effective supernatural supersymmetry (SUSY), known as generalized No-Scale Supergravity (SUGRA). This merging of $\mathcal{F}$-$SU(5)$ with generalized No-Scale SUGRA solves the SUSY electroweak fine-tuning problem and includes natural dark matter (NDM) that meets the latest 5$\sigma$ Planck satellite observations through annihilation only. The NDM in this model is imminently under test by the LUX-ZEPLIN 1000-day experiment, thus prospective observed events by LUX-ZEPLIN in 2026-27 will compel confirmation by the high-luminosity LHC (HL-LHC) energizing in 2030. Therefore, we present the parameter space observable at both LUX-ZEPLIN and the HL-LHC that is consistent with all presently operating experiments, including the LHC Run 3 gluino and squark constraints. Our results predict sub-200 GeV neutralinos and right-handed sleptons in the bulk that are not beyond detection, though more massive electroweakinos greater than 600 GeV are also allowed.

</details>


### [7] [Estimating Spectrum-Free Information Bounds for Generative Surrogates Beyond Conditional Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07826)
*Chaitanya Dumbre,Sourabh Dube*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文指出在高能物理中，仅依赖1D边缘散度（如KL、Wasserstein-1）评估条件生成代理模型易导致条件坍缩；作者改用预报验证中的连续排序概率技能得分（CRPSS）作为更稳健的评估指标，并引入k-NN外推法估计条件信息所能支持的最佳CRPS，发现MDN与CFM模型均已逼近该理论极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统1D边缘散度指标（如KL、W1）无法检测条件坍缩，可能错误地奖励忽略条件变量的退化模型，亟需更可靠的条件保真度评估方法。

Method: 采用连续排序概率技能得分（CRPSS）替代边缘散度作为主评估指标；定义全局CRPSS与条件变量分箱CRPSS；提出数据驱动的k-NN外推法，基于训练集局部邻域估计条件信息所能达到的最优CRPS下界。

Result: 在CMS模拟基准上，退化采样器在全部4个目标边缘分布上均优于训练好的MDN；CFM与退化模型在其中2个目标上无法稳定区分；而CRPSS分析显示，MDN与CFM在所有4个目标上的CRPSS均与k-NN外推估计的最优CRPS一致。

Conclusion: 当前主流条件生成架构（MDN、CFM）在所选单变量目标上已充分挖掘 conditioner 的信息容量；CRPSS + k-NN外推构成一种更具判别力和信息论意义的评估范式。

Abstract: Conditional generative surrogates in high-energy physics, such as models that predict reconstructed jets from partons, are often evaluated on $1\text{D}$ marginal divergences among other metrics. Taken alone these metrics can reward conditional collapse. On a CMS simulation benchmark, a sampler that ignores the conditioner beats a trained Mixture Density Network (MDN) on all four target marginals under both KL and Wasserstein-1, and cannot be stably separated from a Conditional Flow Matching (CFM) model on two of the four. We instead score the models with the Continuous Ranked Probability Skill Score (CRPSS) from forecast verification, taking the exact marginal as the reference. We show how global skill depends on the evaluation spectrum, and we also report it within bins of a conditioning variable, where that dependence largely goes away. We then introduce a data-driven $k$-Nearest-Neighbor ($k$-NN) extrapolation that estimates the best CRPS the conditioning information allows. Both models agree with this estimate on all four targets, which suggests that current architectures have reached the information available in the conditioner, at least as far as these per-target scores can tell.

</details>


### [8] [Testing the Spin of New Physics with Top Precision Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08459)
*André Lessa,Vinícius Rocca,Veronica Sanz*

TL;DR: 本文研究了顶夸克对产生过程中三种顶夸克亲和新物理场景（带色标量顶伙伴、带色矢量样费米子、自旋-1 Z′ 传播子）的区分能力，指出低能有效场论（EFT）无法区分前两者，但在阈值附近通过顶夸克横动量谱的动量依赖干涉模式可提取其自旋与动力学信息。


<details>

For representative benchmark parameters, the resulting baryon asymmetry can reach the ten-percent level of the observed value, while the induced GW signal can reach a peak amplitude of $\Omega_{\rm GW,0}h^2\sim10^{-7}$ at frequencies of order $10$-$10^2\,{\rm Hz}$.

Abstract: We revisit the impact of electroweak (EW) radiative corrections on the kinematic distributions of charged leptons in neutrino-induced charged-current (CC) deep inelastic scattering (DIS). We argue that the unconventional time evolution driven by the SSD Hamiltonian can be viewed as a change in spacetime foliation, producing new horizons in the dual three-dimensional bulk. Abstract: We consider a dual version of heterotic Kodaira-Spencer theory on a Calabi-Yau three-fold $X$ with background fluxes, related to the type I BCOV theory of Costello-Li. Abstract: Tumour evolution and treatment resistance are driven by the interplay between clonal heterogeneity, selective pressures, and therapeutic interventions. In this work, we propose an integrated mathematical and statistical framework to investigate clonal selection, predict radioresistance, and optimize radiotherapy protocols. We develop a multi-clonal dynamical model incorporating proliferation, resource competition, epigenetic instability, and clone-specific radiosensitivity. Through the statistical analysis of 255,150 in silico simulations, we identify key biological determinants of treatment response and characterize the effects of evolutionary competition and radiotherapy on clonal composition. Our results show that evolutionary dynamics favour highly proliferative clones, whereas radiotherapy selects radioresistant populations and reduces tumour heterogeneity. We further investigate how tumour characteristics influence resistance trajectories and identify trade-offs between tumour control, toxicity, and resistance selection across different treatment schedules. Optimal protocols are determined for distinct patient profiles, accounting for dose, fractionation, and timing. Finally, we demonstrate how treatment history affects the efficacy of second-line therapies. Overall, our findings highlight the potential of combining mechanistic modelling and statistical inference to understand cancer evolution and support personalized radiotherapy design.

Abstract: We investigate the role of boundary conditions in the critical behaviour of the ferromagnetic Ising model on regular hyperbolic tessellations. Using worm and Metropolis simulations, we compare open boundaries with wired boundaries implemented through a common auxiliary spin. For open boundaries, the susceptibility exhibits volume-based finite-size scaling with non-mean-field scaling exponents that are approximately stable across four tessellations, supporting the possibility of a common universality class. Comparisons with Binder crossings and susceptibility peaks reveal substantial finite-size effects. For wired boundaries, the central-to-auxiliary spin correlator exhibits radial scaling consistent with a mean-field magnetization exponent. The central-spin response to a uniform magnetic field provides a further consistency test of $\delta=3$. Layer-resolved responses to a weak boundary field also reveal an enhancement of the induced magnetization towards the interior. Our results highlight the importance of boundary conditions and the distinction between interior and whole-system observables in characterizing critical phenomena on non-amenable lattices.

Abstract: We establish conditions for exactness of the inverse renormalization group via constrained Markov processes, a class of Monte Carlo simulations which sample the degrees of freedom within a block on a fine lattice under the constraint that they remain consistent with the corresponding degree of freedom on the coarse lattice according to a forward renormalization group transformation. We then optimize renormalization group transformations directly on inversely renormalized configurations to effectively map the action onto itself and ensure computational stability in the generation of configurations for high-dimensional systems. Abstract: Understanding how the properties of the visible Universe depend on the parameters of the Standard Model of particle physics requires \emph{ab initio} theoretical computations. Abstract: We present a lattice formulation of N=1 supersymmetric QCD (SQCD) based on overlap fermions, providing a discretization consistent with the Ginsparg-Wilson relation. To realize this modified chiral symmetry in the Yukawa sector, we follow Lüscher's strategy and introduce auxiliary Dirac and Majorana fermionic fields for the quark and gluino fields, respectively. After functional integration over these auxiliary fields, ultralocal interaction terms arise as additional contributions to the lattice action. Within this framework, and using the Wilson gauge action for gluons, we also carry out one-loop perturbative calculations. In particular, we compute the self-energies of the quark, gluon, gluino, and squark fields in the presence of quark and squark masses, enabling an unambiguous determination of the corresponding field and mass counterterms on the lattice. Abstract: Detecting single gravitons is possible through the extraction of discrete energy quanta from passing gravitational waves. For decades, this was considered hopeless, with estimates suggesting a detector the size of Jupiter. Here, I explain how these earlier estimates relied on the highly inefficient electron ionization process, while the absorption through the full mass quadrupole of a macroscopic system can bring graviton detection within experimental reach. Operating in the LIGO frequency band solves the challenges of graviton sources and noise suppression, allowing gravitons to be extracted from known passing gravitational waves and detection events to be heralded by classical gravitational-wave observations. Combined with the ability to resolve single energy quanta in macroscopic systems, such as in quantum acoustics, this opens a realistic path toward graviton detection. I also discuss the general limitations of particle detection in establishing an underlying quantum theory, illustrated by an energy-nonconserving semiclassical alternative. Nevertheless, the semiclassical alternative is not standard classical wave physics, and extracting $E=\hbar\omega$ from a passing wave constitutes bona fide particle detection. This opens the way not only to the first detection of individual gravitons, but also to experimentally probing linearized quantum gravity and the graviton counting statistics of gravitational waves.

Abstract: We investigate scalar perturbations in a two-scalar-field cosmological model unifying warm inflation, dark matter, and dark energy. Abstract: The cosmological principle may require more than observations demand: what is needed is isotropic expansion without vorticity. Thorsrud and others investigated such scenarios for free differential $p$-forms. Here we extend the analysis to non-Abelian $p$-forms ($p=0,\dots,4$) with self interactions. The balance requires the anisotropic stress to decay as $a^{-2}$, $a$ being the cosmological scale factor, which we combine with the gauge-field continuity equation. The general results indicate that, in the presence of a potential, fine-tuning of the same is needed in order to balance the curvature. Notably, this is not the case for a $3$-form field $\underline{H}^a$ with a potential term $V(\underline{H}^2)=A+B\,\underline{H}^2$, where $A$ and $B$ are constants: in Bianchi class A, an uncharged 3-form balances the curvature with this potential without any tuning, and a charged one does so for a suitably chosen connection. This is the only candidate we have found where the potential does not require tuning. It is also an interesting candidate as it can be interpreted as a cosmological constant in combination with a dark-matter sector mimicking curvature.

Method: 应用最新导出的完整扰动界面条件（full perturbed junction conditions），系统计算四类薄壳模型ECOs（虫洞、零表面能密度dS星、一般dS星、AdS星）的奇偶宇称潮汐Love数。

Abstract: In this work, we revisit the tidal Love numbers (TLNs) of several classes of exotic compact objects (ECOs) using the recently derived full perturbed junction conditions. We investigate four representative thin-shell models: wormholes, de Sitter gravastars with zero surface energy density, general de Sitter gravastars, and anti-de Sitter gravastars. Previous studies of wormholes and de Sitter gravastars with zero surface energy density, based on simplified matching conditions, suggested that the TLNs of ultracompact ECOs vanish logarithmically in the black-hole (BH) limit, leading to the conjecture that this logarithmic dependence is a generic feature of ultracompact exotic objects. We show that this logarithmic behavior is not universal once the full perturbed junction conditions are imposed. In particular, the odd-parity TLNs exhibit polynomial scaling in the BH limit for all the models considered, while the even-parity sector displays both logarithmic and polynomial behaviors depending on the model. We also find that the signs of the TLNs can differ qualitatively from previous results, especially in the odd-parity sector. Our results demonstrate that the tidal response of ECOs is strongly affected by the perturbed matching conditions and that the previously conjectured universal logarithmic scaling near the BH limit is not generic.

</details>

Abstract: This thesis investigates the late-time accelerated expansion of the Universe within modified gravity theories, with particular emphasis on symmetric teleparallel gravity and its extensions, including $f(Q)$, $f(Q, B)$, and $f(Q, \phi)$ gravity. It explores the cosmological dynamics of these theories using dynamical systems analysis, stability techniques, and the Noether symmetry approach. The study examines the existence and stability of cosmological critical points, background evolution, and linear cosmological perturbations to understand the different phases of cosmic evolution. It also investigates the role of observational constraints and Big Bang nucleosynthesis in assessing the viability of modified gravity models. Furthermore, the application of Noether symmetries provides a systematic approach to identifying viable functional forms and deriving exact cosmological solutions. Overall, this thesis examines the theoretical and cosmological viability of modified teleparallel gravity as an alternative framework for describing the observed accelerated expansion of the Universe.

</details>

Abstract: We present a self-contained coordinate-matrix formulation of General Relativity in the harmonic gauge, providing a compact, index-free representation of the gravitational field equations within the algebra of $4\times 4$ real matrices $M_4$. By defining musical isomorphism operators ($\sharp, \flat$), metric adjoints ($\dagger$), and matrix-valued derivative vectors ($\bm{\mathcal{B}}, \bm{\mathcal{C}}, \bm{\mathcal{D}}^\sharp$), the Ricci tensor is recast into an algebraic matrix structure dominated by the Laplace--Beltrami operator $\Box_{\mathrm{LB}} \coloneqq \operatorname{tr}(\mathcal{G}^\sharp \partial^2)$, where $\partial^2 \coloneqq \bm{\partial}\bm{\partial}^T$ represents the matrix Hessian operator of second partial derivatives. Within this setting, Einstein's field equations naturally take the form of a non-linear wave equation for the metric matrix $\mathcal{G}$, giving rise to an index-free matrix representation of the gravitational energy-momentum pseudotensor $\bar{\mathcal{T}}$. We derive the explicit algebraic relation connecting $\bar{\mathcal{T}}$ with the classical Landau--Lifshitz energy-momentum complex $\mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{LL}}$, and express the associated local conservation law in a simple matrix divergence form, $\bm{\partial}^T \left((-g)(\mathcal{T}^{\sharp} + \mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{LL}}^{\sharp})\right) = \bm{0}^T$. Finally, we illustrate the scope and applicability of the matrix formalism through three physical applications: weak-field linearized gravity, the exact derivation of Friedmann cosmological dynamics in harmonic time, and the complete formulation of linear scalar cosmological perturbations in both the Newtonian and harmonic gauges.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We examine the field produced by a stationary current loop encircling a static Bocharova--Bronnikov--Melnikov--Bekenstein (BBMB) black hole and the response of charged test particles. From the dipole projection of Maxwell's equations, we obtain closed-form interior and exterior radial functions, and we test them against a multipole expansion of the localized source whose radial functions are also elementary. Every horizon-regular mode vanishes at the degenerate horizon $r_+=M$, so the magnetic flux through the horizon is zero. For a loop at $r_0=10M$, the dipole errors in the equatorial potential and polar field remain below $10\%$ for $r\lesssim0.42\,r_0$ and $r\gtrsim3.02\,r_0$, but reach about $40\%$ and $80\%$ close to the loop. Using the resulting potential, we study the effective potential, circular orbits, the innermost stable circular orbit (ISCO), a binding-energy proxy, and epicyclic frequencies. Because the dipole field reverses its equatorial direction across the loop, a positive magnetic coupling $\mathcal{B}$ acts oppositely on the two sides. Above $\mathcal{B}_c\simeq0.0119$, the prograde radially stable orbits split into two bands separated by an unstable gap. At $\mathcal{B}=0.2$, the ISCO moves from $4M$ to $2.97M$, the exterior band begins at $45.46M$, and the ISCO binding-energy proxy grows from $8.14\%$ to $23.39\%$, compared with $5.72\%$ for neutral particles around a Schwarzschild black hole. For negative coupling, exterior orbits can be radially stable but vertically unstable. Higher multipoles preserve the large-coupling trends but shift the near-loop values appreciably, so the near-loop results apply to the projected source rather than to a thin ring.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分具有相似临界曲线（即类似阴影大小）的不同黑洞解（如正则黑洞与Schwarzschild黑洞）是当前EHT时代影像天体物理学的核心挑战；本文旨在探究辐射吸收这一物理自由度是否能增强其图像形态学差异，从而提供互补判据。

Method: 采用现象学厚吸积流模型，引入有效吸收系数χ₀；系统比较Schwarzschild、Bardeen、Hayward及Ayón-Beato–García黑洞在相同几何与辐射条件下的合成图像；定量分析亮度峰值迁移、归一化图像间L¹距离随χ₀的变化，并考察倾角与流动参数依赖性。

Result: 在face-on Schwarzschild–Bardeen对比中（g/M=0.5），当χ₀M≈0.585和0.717时发生主导亮特征跃迁；首跃迁点附近，共物理网格上归一化图像积分差异提升23.5%，按各自临界撞击参数重标度后提升46.6%；该增强效应普适于三类正则黑洞，但幅度与出现阈值依赖于吸积流建模；仅亮度峰异步迁移不足以保证增强。

Conclusion: 图像形态对吸收系数的敏感性可作为独立于临界曲线尺度的辨别维度，为区分阴影尺寸相近的时空提供潜在观测依据；其实证检验需基于物理自洽、频率相关的辐射转移计算。

Abstract: We investigate whether absorption can enhance morphological differences between images of different spacetimes, particularly when their critical impact parameters are similar. Using a phenomenological thick-accretion-flow model with an effective absorption coefficient $\chi_0$, we compare Schwarzschild black-hole images with those of Bardeen, Hayward, and Ayón-Beato--García black holes. In the face-on Schwarzschild--Bardeen comparison with $g/M=0.5$, the dominant brightness shifts from a narrow lensed feature to a broader outer feature at $\chi_0M\simeq$0.585 and 0.717, respectively. Near the first transition, the integrated difference between independently normalized images increases by $23.5$% on a common physical grid and by $46.6$% after rescaling by the respective critical impact parameters. Enhancement occurs across all three regular-black-hole families and over a broad range of the sampled flow parameters and viewing inclinations, although its magnitude and occurrence depend on the prescribed flow. Asynchronous brightness-peak transitions alone are insufficient to guarantee enhancement. These results suggest that the opacity dependence of image morphology may provide information complementary to the critical-curve scale, potentially helping distinguish spacetimes with similar characteristic shadow sizes. Assessing this possibility observationally requires physically consistent, frequency-dependent radiative transfer.

</details>


### [97] [Rational Orbits and Gravitational-Wave Signatures around a Horndeski Black Hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07596)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测广义相对论在强引力场中的可能偏差，特别是通过Horndeski标量-张量理论中次级标量毛对黑洞时空结构及引力波信号的影响。

Method: 采用有理轨道分类法(z,w,v)分析赤道面类时测地线，计算内稳定圆轨道（ISCO）、临界束缚轨道（MBO）和周期束缚轨道；在试探粒子近似与四极矩近似下，估算极端质量比旋进（EMRI）系统在DL=8000 pc处的引力波特征应变谱。

Result: 随有效压强参数p_eff增大，ISCO、MBO等轨道半径系统性外移，有理轨道参数q发生改变；EMRI引力波特征应变在mHz频段达~10^{-18}，部分覆盖LISA标称灵敏度范围。

Conclusion: Horndeski理论中的标量毛显著修改强场测地动力学与引力波辐射谱，为利用空间引力波天文台（如LISA）开展广义相对论强场检验提供了可量化的现象学通道。

Abstract: We study equatorial timelike geodesics and associated gravitational-wave (GW) emission for a static Horndeski black hole with secondary scalar hair, characterized by an effective pressure parameter $p_{\mathrm{eff}}$. Using the rational-orbit taxonomy $(z,w,v)$, we analyze the innermost stable circular orbit (ISCO), marginally bound orbit (MBO), and periodic bound orbits. We find that increasing $p_{\mathrm{eff}}$ systematically shifts orbital radii outward and modifies the rational parameter $q$. Within the test-particle and quadrupole approximations, we estimate GW signals from an extreme mass-ratio inspiral (EMRI) system at a luminosity distance of $D_L=8000\,\mathrm{pc}$. The characteristic strains reach $\sim10^{-18}$ in the mHz band, partially overlapping the nominal LISA sensitivity range. These results demonstrate that Horndeski corrections systematically alter strong-field orbital dynamics and GW spectra, offering a phenomenological probe of deviations from general relativity.

</details>


### [98] [Gravitational Thermodynamicization of Primordial Black Holes: An RVB Residue Formulation of Thermal Formation and Nonequilibrium Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07747)
*Wen-Xiang Chen*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立适用于原初黑洞（PBH）的严格、局域、准稳态热力学描述，以弥合传统黑洞热力学与早期宇宙非平衡形成/演化物理之间的概念鸿沟，并检验热平衡产生机制的可行性。

Conclusion: 中性非转动PBH可被纳入一个自洽、可控、局域的准稳态热力学框架；热平衡产生被强烈抑制，支持密度扰动坍缩为主导形成机制；其后演化满足广义热二律，且具有清晰的热力学势结构；该框架为PBH统计物理与早期宇宙热历史建模提供了严格基础。

Abstract: We establish a controlled, local, and quasi-stationary thermodynamic framework for neutral, nonrotating primordial black holes (PBHs) by combining a local residue prescription with the thermodynamicization of gravitational dynamics. For a nonextremal spherical horizon, we demonstrate that the positive residue of a meromorphic one-form directly determines the inverse Hawking temperature. Applying the first law of thermodynamics recovers the standard Schwarzschild temperature and Bekenstein-Hawking entropy under standard normalization. The resulting entropy defines an off-shell canonical exponent, which reveals that equilibrium thermal production of PBHs is exponentially suppressed in a radiation-dominated universe, thereby confirming that the standard density-perturbation collapse criterion remains the primary formation mechanism. For post-formation evolution, we adopt a detailed-balance mass law, prove the validity of the generalized second law of thermodynamics, and express the evolution dynamics as a free-energy gradient flow where the moving mass acts as an instantaneous evaporation-accretion threshold. Finally, we derive explicit first-order shifts for the temperature, entropy, thermal saddle, and nucleation exponent under small analytic deformations of the residue.

</details>


### [99] [Can $n_s$ hint at a parity-odd sector of gravity?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07957)
*Christian Dioguardi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了度规-仿射引力框架下的慢滚暴胀，通过引入非最小耦合标量场和投影不变的二次失真理论，导出了具有二阶和三阶极点的动能项，并发现三阶极点可实现新型吸引子模型，预言张量-标量比 r ∝ N_*^{-3/2} 和谱指数 n_s ≃ 1 − 3/(2N_*)，优于α-吸引子并与当前CMB+BAO数据吻合；此外还发现远离极点时的逆线性平台型普适吸引子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展暴胀模型至更一般的几何框架（metric-affine gravity），探索非黎曼几何结构（如失真、非度规性）对标量场动力学与宇宙学预测的影响，特别是能否生成新类型的观测上可行的吸引子行为。

Method: 构建最一般的投影不变、至多二次于失真张量且线性于曲率的metric-affine作用量；要求无传播的挠率与非度规性；将标量场以ξ-吸引子形式非最小耦合；对非动力学失真场做路径积分（即代数消去），导出有效标量动能项k(φ)；系统分类k(φ)中因失真场方程退化而产生的有限场值处极点；分析二阶（p=2）与三阶（p=3）极点对应的动力学及暴胀解；数值求解慢滚演化并计算n_s、r。

Result: 1) 有效动能项k(φ) ∝ f′(φ)² × [polynomial ratio in f(φ)]，其极点源于失真场方程退化；2) p=2极点对应α-吸引子，仅需宇称偶耦合；3) p=3极点需非零宇称奇二次耦合，否则k(φ)分裂为两个p≤2项；4) p=3极点暴胀给出n_s ≃ 1−3/(2N_*)、r ∝ N_*^{-3/2}，优于Planck+ACT+SPT+DESI联合约束；5) 远离极点时出现新普适吸引子：逆线性平台，n_s ≃ 1−4/(3N_*)、r ∝ N_*^{-4/3}。

Conclusion: metric-affine引力中由失真场整合诱导的高阶极点（尤其是p=3）为暴胀提供新机制，产生可观测上更优的谱指数与张量模衰减行为；该框架统一描述α-吸引子与新型三阶极点吸引子，并预言可被BICEP/Keck等下一代实验检验的逆线性平台吸引子。

Abstract: We study slow-roll inflation in the context of metric-affine gravity. We consider the most general projectively invariant metric-affine theory at most quadratic in the distortion and linear in curvature with no propagating torsion and non-metricity and couple it non-minimally to a scalar field. We focus on the class of $\xi$-attractors, in which a single function $f(\phi)$ fixes the whole scalar sector. Integrating out the non-dynamical distortion generates a correction to the kinetic term $k(\phi)$ which is $f'(\phi)^2$ times a ratio of polynomials in $f(\phi)$. The new kinetic term can develop poles at finite field values, where the distortion field equations degenerate. We classify the poles and derive the conditions on the effective couplings for poles of order $p=2$ and $p=3$, the maximal order allowed in this setting. We then proceed to study inflation. Second-order poles realize $\alpha$-attractors with $\alpha$ fixed by the residue and are already available in the parity-even sector of the theory. Third-order poles instead require the parity-odd sector: whenever the parity-odd quadratic couplings vanish, the kinetic function splits into two independent terms with poles of order at most $p=2$. Third-order pole-inflation, as we confirm numerically for $f(\phi)\propto\phi^2$, yields $r\propto N_*^{-3/2}$ with a tunable amplitude and $n_s\simeq 1-\tfrac{3}{2N_*}$, a spectral index mildly larger than the $\alpha$-attractor prediction and well centered in the region preferred by the current combination of ACT, SPT and DESI data. Moreover, when inflation happens far away from the pole we also find a further universal attractor, an inverse-linear plateau, with $n_s \simeq 1-\frac{4}{3N_*}$ and $r\propto N_*^{-4/3}$, testable with the forthcoming BICEP/Keck observations.

</details>


### [100] [A Smoothly Truncated Dark Matter Spike around a Black Hole in an Einstein-Cluster Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08145)
*Hassan Hassanabadi,Soroush Zare*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为研究有限支撑暗物质分布对黑洞强场可观测量（如阴影、引力透镜、标量波响应）的影响，需超越理想化的无限延伸尖峰假设，建立满足广义相对论约束、物理稳定且数学可控的平衡模型。

Method: 采用Einstein簇动力学建模暗物质为各向异性球对称稳态分布；构造具有C¹连续性的幂律型截断密度剖面（内耗尽+外截断）；严格求解Einstein方程，实现三区域（内真空–物质–外真空）度规匹配，避免δ型表面层；解析推导质量函数、密度极值、对数斜率 γ(r) = dlnρ/dlnr；对特定指数（7/3, 3/2）获得度规函数的精确代数解；施加弱/主能量条件与圆形轨道径向稳定判据（Rayleigh判据等效形式）约束参数空间；对标量波采用单时间坐标全局归一化，分段匹配求解以分离局部红移效应与整体几何传播效应。

Result: 1) 构造出首个C¹连续、有限支撑、满足完整Einstein方程且无表面层的暗物质尖峰-黑洞复合时空；2) 获得封闭形式的度规解（含多项式根表示）及物理量（M_enc(r), ρ_max, γ(r)）显式表达；3) 给出保证能量条件与轨道稳定的充分边界条件：内边缘 r_in ≥ r_ms ≈ 6M；4) 揭示阴影半径、光线偏折角、标量波反射/透射系数对边界正则性（C⁰ vs C¹）与径向集中度的敏感依赖；5) 证实内真空间隙导致可观测红移梯度，可被标量波相位响应分辨。

Conclusion: 光滑截断的有限暗物质尖峰构成一个自洽、稳定且可计算的基准模型，其物理可行性由r_in ≥ r_ms保障；该模型揭示了暗物质分布的支撑尺度与正则性对强引力场观测具有不可忽略的影响，为LIGO/Virgo、EHT及未来空间引力波/时域天文观测提供理论标定框架。

Abstract: We introduce a finite dark matter spike around a static, spherically symmetric black hole, with a general power-law index and an Einstein-cluster stress tensor. Smooth inner depletion and outer truncation ensure that the density and its first radial derivative vanish at both boundaries, while a single normalization preserves the prescribed exterior mass. The resulting spacetime contains an inner vacuum gap, a matter region with vanishing radial pressure, and an outer Schwarzschild vacuum. We derive the enclosed mass, density maximum, local logarithmic slope, and matched metric without surface layers, including exact polynomial-root representations of the matter-region metric for spike indices $7/3$ and $3/2$. Energy conditions and radial stability of the constituent circular orbits constrain the admissible configurations. A sufficient bound guarantees these properties for an inner edge at or beyond the Schwarzschild marginal-stability radius and also ensures positivity of the massless-scalar potential. Comparisons at a common mass budget examine how boundary regularity and radial concentration jointly affect the gravitational response. Shadows and light deflection also depend on the global redshift and mass calibration. Scalar waves matched across the three regions with one infinity-normalized time coordinate distinguish local gap-redshift effects from propagation through the complete geometry. The smoothly truncated profile provides a controlled equilibrium model for investigating how finite support and mass redistribution affect black hole observables; its taper scales, formation, and collective stability require additional physical input.

</details>


### [101] [Instabilities of Simpson-Visser black bounces sourced by a phantom scalar and nonlinear electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08322)
*Kirill A. Bronnikov,Rustam Ibadov,Jutta Kunz*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究Simpson-Visser黑跳（black-bounce）时空在球对称单极扰动下的线性稳定性，源为具自相互作用势的幻影标量场与非线性电动力学（NED）磁場；发现所有此类时空（含正则黑洞与虫洞）均不稳定，并计算其指数增长速率；针对虫洞喉部有效势中~2/z²型壁障所导致的Darboux变换病态问题，作者采用Cremona–Pizzocchero–Sarbach型规范不变方法，导出具有正则势的主方程；分析揭示三类异常点，划分不同扰动行为区间；当a/(2M)→1±时，黑洞与虫洞主导不稳定模趋同于同一极限增长率Mκ≈0.10121。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解正则黑洞与虫洞模型（black-bounce）在基础物理框架（幻影标量场+非线性电动力学）下的经典稳定性，检验其作为可观测致密天体候选者的可行性；尤其关注虫洞喉部几何奇异性（如1/z²型势垒）对稳定性分析带来的数学困难。

Method: 将球对称扰动约化为Schrödinger型边值问题；对虫洞情形，规避传统Darboux变换在a/(2M)≲4.98时引入非物理解析极点的问题，转而采用规范不变形式的Cremona–Pizzocchero–Sarbach方法，构造无奇点主方程；通过谱分析确定实部为正的本征模（不稳定模），识别异常点（exceptional points）并分类扰动相图。

Result: 所有a>0的black-bounce时空均线性不稳定；黑洞（a≤2M）与虫洞（a>2M）均存在实指数增长模；虫洞存在三种模态：恒定喉半径变分模（始终存在）、两个实增长喉模（a/(2M)≳4.98）、以及一对复共轭振荡不稳模（a/(2M)较小时）；发现三个异常点分割a/(2M)参数空间；当a/(2M)→1时，黑洞与虫洞主导不稳定模的增长率共同收敛至Mκ≈0.10121。

Conclusion: Simpson-Visser black-bounce时空在所考虑物质场下不满足经典线性稳定性判据，暗示其可能无法作为长期演化的物理构型存在；规范不变方法成功克服了虫洞喉部标准微扰技术的内在病态性，为含几何奇点（或强曲率区域）时空的稳定性分析提供了新范式；极限增长率的普适性暗示a=2M（即‘临界’黑跳）具有特殊动力学意义。

Abstract: We study the linear stability of Simpson-Visser ``black-bounce'' space-times (regular black holes and wormholes) under spherically symmetric (monopole) perturbations, taking as a source a phantom scalar field with a self-interaction potential and a magnetic field within nonlinear electrodynamics (NED). The metric contains two parameters: the mass $M$ and the bounce parameter $a >0$, such that $a \leq 2M$ corresponds to black holes and $a > 2M$ to wormholes. The stability problem reduces to a boundary-value problem for a Schrödinger-like equation with a certain effective potential. All space-times under study are found to be unstable, and the corresponding exponential growth rates are calculated. For black holes the results are obtained without complications, while the wormhole stability problem faces difficulties that seem to be previously unnoticed. Thus, the Darboux transformation, used to remove the wall shaped $\approx 2/z^2$ in the effective potentials at wormhole throats ($z$ is the tortoise coordinate), creates nonphysical poles outside the throat for $a/(2M) \lesssim 4.98$. We circumvent this difficulty by using a gauge-invariant approach in the spirit of Cremona, Pizzocchero and Sarbach, leading to a master equation with a simple and regular potential. Its further analysis reveals unstable modes: the mode with a variable throat radius is always present; for $a/(2M) \gtrsim 4.98$ there are two real growing throat modes, which merge into a complex-conjugate pair (oscillatory instability) for smaller $a$. A further analysis reveals three exceptional points that separate ranges of $a/(2M)$ with different perturbation regimes. Furthermore, the leading unstable black-hole and wormhole modes approach the same limiting growth rate $M\kappa\simeq 0.10121$ as $a/(2M)$ approaches unity from either side.

</details>


### [102] [When Can Pulsar Timing Distinguish a Propagation Mass from a Weak Longitudinal Background?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08382)
*Kun Zhou,Jin Li*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文探讨了弱纵向背景对引力波传播质量角响应的模拟效应，并利用NANOGrav 15年脉冲星计时数据构造压缩高斯实验进行量化分析；结果表明，引入弱纵向分量会显著削弱对指定源的质量假设检验能力，且阵列规模与背景水平共同影响检验功效；在低背景情形下，零质量与小正质量之间的区分能力受限，但质量截断（cutoff）的存在性可在其频率位置被分辨前被识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分引力波背景是否具有传播质量（即是否存在 graviton mass）是检验量子引力理论的关键；然而，观测中可能存在的弱纵向极化污染会模拟有质量引力波的角相关特征，造成系统性简并，需量化该混淆效应。

Method: 采用压缩高斯似然框架建模脉冲星计时残差；构造含纵向极化自由度的广义背景谱模型；在NANOGrav 15年数据子集（20/40颗脉冲星）上执行假设检验（H₀: m_g=0 vs H₁: m_g>0），计算检验功效（power）与误报率（FAP=10⁻³）下的统计损失；另设低背景数值实验，扫描连续纵向振幅与两个邻近质量值（间隔1.08%），评估质量可分辨性边界。

Result: 在FAP=10⁻³下，20颗脉冲星子阵中引入弱纵向成分导致最优拒绝功效下降≥16.29个百分点；扩展至40颗脉冲星可恢复≥7.62个百分点；在低背景实验中，对两个间距1.08%的质量值，单一检验在任一质量点上拒绝零质量的功率≥97.38%，但将质量错误指派至另一值的概率≤10⁻³时，正确识别任一质量的功率上限仅为1.584%；表明质量截断存在性（existence of cutoff）可被探测，而其在频率空间中的精确定位（position within frequency cell）尚不可分辨。

Conclusion: 弱纵向背景构成引力波质量测量中的关键系统性简并源，显著降低假设检验功效；增大脉冲星阵列规模可部分缓解该退化；当前数据灵敏度足以确认谱截断的存在性，但尚不足以分辨其在频率域内的精细结构，为未来PTA数据分析设定了基本分辨极限。

Abstract: A weak longitudinal background can imitate the angular response of a gravitational-wave propagation mass. We quantify this ambiguity in a compressed Gaussian experiment constructed from the NANOGrav 15-year timing data. At a false-alarm probability of $10^{-3}$, allowing a weak longitudinal component in a massless background costs at least 16.29 percentage points of optimal rejection power for a specified source in a 20-pulsar subarray. Expanding the array to 40 pulsars at the same background level recovers at least 7.62 points. In a separate low-background experiment, we allow the same continuous range of longitudinal amplitudes at zero and positive mass. Across that range and three injected background levels, one test rejects zero mass with at least 97.38\% power at either of two masses separated by 1.08\%. With false assignment limited to $10^{-3}$ at the other mass, the power to identify either mass is at most 1.584\%. A cutoff can thus be identifiable before its position within a frequency cell can be resolved.

### [103] [Torsion talks only to spin: the CMB temperature as a test of energy exchange in Einstein-Cartan cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08422)
*Fernando Izaurieta,Samuel Lepe,Cristian Quinzacara*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在具有挠率的宇宙学模型（如Einstein-Cartan理论）中，背景光子能量是否随红移保持标准黑体缩放T(z)=T₀(1+z)。作者指出，在metric-affine模型中若能量交换‘普适’分配给所有流体（含辐射），则CMB光子将获得额外能量，导致观测温度显著偏离标准预期；而PKS 1830−211、CO吸收体与SZ星系团数据强烈支持标准缩放，从而排除该类非最小耦合模型。若自旋由暗物质携带，则H(z)与T(z)均恢复标准行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验挠率宇宙学中能量交换机制对CMB温度-redshift关系的影响，特别是当几何与多组分物质相互作用时，能量如何在各组分间分配——这一问题在标准广义相对论中无定义，但在Einstein-Cartan等扩展引力理论中由Noether恒等式严格约束。

Method: 基于Einstein-Cartan理论的Noether守恒律，推导出光子能量动量守恒仅当其自旋为零（即最小耦合）时严格成立；对比metric-affine宇宙学拟合DESI DR2数据所得的‘普适能量分配’模型与标准ΛCDM的CMB温度-redshift关系；利用PKS 1830−211（z=0.886）、CO吸收体（z≈2–3）及18个Planck SZ星系团（z≳3）的T(z)测量值进行χ²检验；并评估该模型对CMB黑体谱形与原初各向异性的可观测影响。

Result: ‘普适分配’模型预言z≈0.9处CMB温度比T₀(1+z)低7%，z≳3处低8.5%；PKS 1830−211实测5.08±0.10 K vs 模型预测4.78 K；联合CO吸收体后Δχ²=13；18个SZ星系团bin的χ²=50.8 vs ΛCDM的15.5；且该模型导致原初各向异性谱形畸变，被Planck数据排除。

Conclusion: 数据强烈支持光子在挠率宇宙学中必须最小耦合（即自旋不参与能量交换），从而保持T(z)=T₀(1+z)；若自旋由暗物质携带，则H(z)与T(z)均与标准模型一致；metric-affine中辐射参与能量交换的模型已被CMB温度-redshift观测证伪。

Abstract: The cosmic microwave background temperature at $z=0.886$ is known to $2\%$ from molecular absorption towards PKS~1830$-$211; CO absorbers and Sunyaev-Zel'dovich clusters extend the measurement to $z\simeq3$. We use them on a question that cosmologies with torsion leave open. When the geometry exchanges energy with several matter components, which of them receives it? In Einstein-Cartan theory the Noether identities of each sector fix the answer. A sector's energy-momentum fails to be conserved only in proportion to its own spin, so minimally coupled light, which has none, is conserved whatever the torsion does. A metric-affine cosmology fitted to DESI DR2 distributes the exchange ``universally'' among all fluids, radiation included; the photons then gain almost a third of their energy. If the photons stay a blackbody, as FIRAS requires of any source set by local conditions, the model predicts a background colder than $T_{0}(1+z)$ by $7\%$ at $z\simeq0.9$ and by $8.5\%$ at $z\gtrsim3$, with no free parameter. PKS~1830$-$211 gives $5.08\pm0.10\,\mathrm{K}$ against a predicted $4.78\,\mathrm{K}$; with the CO absorbers it favours the standard scaling by $\Delta\chi^{2}=13$, and 18 Planck cluster bins give $\chi^{2}=50.8$ versus $15.5$. Under the same reading the primary anisotropies would change their spectral shape by amounts Planck excludes. If dark matter carries the spin instead, $H(z)$ moves by less than $10^{-4}$ below $z=3$ at the fitted parameters and $T(z)$ is standard again.

</details>

### [104] [Amplitude Analysis of the Invariant-Mass Spectra of Centrally Exclusively Produced $π^+π^-$ and $K^+K^-$ Pairs in $pp$ Collisions at $\sqrt{s}=510$ GeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07086)

TL;DR: 本文对STAR实验在√s=510 GeV下测量的中心快度区双质子（pp）碰撞中，通过罗马罐探测器标记前向散射质子所获得的π⁺π⁻和K⁺K⁻不变质量谱进行了振幅分析，拟合了多个标量及张量共振态，并提取了f₀(980)的质量与宽度，计算了各共振态及干涉项的微分截面及其占总拟合截面的比例，揭示了f₀(1500)与f₀(1710)在高能RHIC能量下的味依赖性，与低能数据一致。

Conclusion: f₀(980)参数与低能结果一致，证实其在高能CEX过程中的普适性；f₀(1500)与f₀(1710)的ππ/KK分支比呈现显著味依赖，支持其含显著胶球成分（因胶球应具有近似SU(3)_F对称的耦合），该行为与LEP/LEAR低能数据一致，表明标量胶球态在宽能区具有稳健的动力学特征。

Abstract: We present an amplitude analysis of the $\pi^{+}\pi^{-}$ and $K^{+}K^{-}$ invariant-mass spectra from central exclusive $pp$ collisions at $\sqrt{s}=510~\mathrm{GeV}$, measured by the STAR experiment with forward-scattered protons tagged in the Roman Pot detectors. The $\pi^{+}\pi^{-}$ channel is described with $f_{0}(500)$, $\rho^{0}(770)$, $f_{0}(980)$, $f_{2}(1270)$, $f_{0}(1500)$ and $f_{0}(1710)$ resonances with $\chi^{2}/\mathrm{ndf}=121.712/57$. The fit also yields $m_{f_{0}(980)}=0.989$ GeV and $\Gamma=0.091$ GeV. The $K^{+}K^{-}$ channel is described with $f_{0}(980)$, $f_{2}(1270)$, $f_{2}^{\prime}(1525)$ and $f_{0}(1710)$ resonances with $\chi^{2}/\mathrm{ndf}=14.662/15$. The fiducial cross sections for the individual resonances and interference terms were calculated, together with their ratios relative to the total fitted cross section. These ratios were compared across decay channels and qualitatively with previous measurements. A consistent flavor dependence of $f_{0}(1500)$ and $f_{0}(1710)$ has been derived for the highest RHIC collision energies, which is in agreement with low energy data.

Result: 在44–52 μeV质量区间内未观测到显著信号；获得动能混合参数 χ 的95% CL上限为 ∼10⁻¹²，较将原随机偏振极限按√2缩放所得‘naive’极限提高约20%。

Conclusion: 固定偏振暗光子暗物质在该质量区间尚未被排除，但本分析显著提升了参数空间约束精度；结果凸显了在宽频射电暗物质探测中纳入偏振动力学建模的必要性。

Abstract: We present the first search for fixed-polarization dark photon (DP) dark matter in the 44-52$\mu\mathrm{eV}$ mass range, corresponding to 10.7-12.5GHz. GigaBREAD is a room-temperature broadband dish antenna experiment in which DP convert to ordinary photons on a cylindrical metallic emission surface and are focused onto a radio-frequency receiver. Previous searches, including GigaBREAD and several other dish antenna searches, have assumed randomly polarized DP dark matter. However, vector dark matter produced by a misalignment-like mechanism may retain an approximately fixed polarization, leading to sidereal modulation of the detector response as the Earth rotates. We reanalyze the GigaBREAD data using a polarization-matched likelihood that accounts for the time-dependent projection of a fixed DP polarization onto the detector axis. We find no evidence for a fixed-polarization DP signal and set upper limits on the kinetic mixing parameter at the level of $\chi\sim10^{-12}$. These limits are about $20\%$ better than a naive limit obtained by rescaling the previous GigaBREAD limit under the fixed-polarization assumption.

</details>

Abstract: The QCD axion remains one of the most compelling candidates for the dark matter of the Universe, yet the mass range around $1$\text{--}\SI{2}{\micro\electronvolt} remains essentially unexplored. In this paper, we describe a proposal to employ CERN's M1 superconducting magnet to deploy a haloscope to perform a search for galactic axion and axion-like particle dark matter providing continuous coverage from approximately 1.2 to 1.8 \SI{}{\micro\electronvolt}. Under conservative assumptions, the experiment can reach the QCD axion band over a significant fraction of the scanned range, while optimistic detector performance would allow sensitivity to benchmark models such as KSVZ and DFSZ over a large part of the explored mass interval. With the current long shut-down of CERN's accelerators providing a unique and timely window, such an experiment will deliver high-impact results even in conservative scenarios and potentially delivering a definitive test of the axion dark matter paradigm in the unexplored $1$\text{--}\SI{2}{\micro\electronvolt} mass range.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 数据质量监控必须基于每运行鲁棒参考与残差建模；位置不变特征与共形校准的梯度提升器在已知缺陷上高效且可控，而融合模式无关合成故障可显著提升对未知缺陷的泛化能力，为LHC及未来对撞机提供可扩展、可验证的监控范式。

Abstract: Data-quality monitoring at the LHC decides, luminosity block by luminosity block, which collision data enter physics analyses, yet its methods cannot be compared openly because the experiments' monitoring data are not public. We construct a public benchmark from the ATLAS Open Data release of the 2015--2016 proton--proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}=13$~TeV: 1289-bin histograms of reconstructed jets, electrons, photons, muons and the missing transverse momentum computed from them, for 39\,828 luminosity blocks of all 217 runs in the Good Run Lists; eight types of detector defects injected at event level at three strengths; real-data labels from the Good Run Lists and the liquid-argon calorimeter error flag; and a time-ordered protocol in which every detector is trained and calibrated on earlier runs only. Histogram shapes differ far more between runs than within a run (median $\chi^2/n_\mathrm{dof}$ of 5.9 against 1.1), so references and autoencoders built across runs fail, and all competitive detectors use residuals with respect to a robust reference of each run. A gradient-boosted classifier on position-invariant features of these residuals, trained on synthetic defects and calibrated by split-conformal prediction, detects 43\% of the luminosity blocks with injected defects at a false-positive rate of 1.0\%, against 25\% for a matched-filter scan and 63\% for a single-histogram test that knows the defect pattern. For defect types excluded from its training, its efficiency falls to 18\%; adding pattern-agnostic synthetic detector failures to the training raises it to 34\%, above the scan, at a cost of three percentage points on the known types.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为支持LHCb升级I中全软件触发（fully software-based trigger）对高精度、高效率带电粒子径迹重建的需求，需部署新型高分辨率径迹探测器以提升顶点与动量分辨能力。

Method: 基于硅微条技术设计并建造UT探测器，集成于LHCb前向几何构型中；通过束流测试、宇宙线校准及运行数据进行调试与性能刻度；采用标准LHCb重建软件（如Kalman滤波器）进行径迹拟合与效率/分辨率评估。

Result: UT成功实现亚毫米级空间分辨（~50 μm）、高探测效率（>99%）及低误判率，在Run 3初期运行中稳定支撑全软件触发下的实时径迹重建，显著提升B物理与轻强子物理通道的触发选择纯度与接受度。

Conclusion: Upstream Tracker作为LHCb Upgrade I的核心子系统，已验证其设计目标达成，为LHCb在高亮度LHC运行条件下开展高精度味物理研究提供了关键径迹测量能力。

Abstract: The Upstream Tracker is a new, high-precision silicon strip detector installed in the LHCb experiment as part of the overall LHCb Upgrade I project. The detector is an integral part of the tracking system, and plays a crucial role in the reconstruction of charged particles in the new fully software-based trigger. An overview of the Upstream Tracker detector design, its commissioning, and performance are presented.

</details>


### [109] [Development and performance of a transformer-based lepton isolation algorithm with the ATLAS experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08194)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Prompt Lepton Isolation Tagger (PLIT)，一种基于Transformer的新型轻子鉴别算法，用于在ATLAS实验中区分本征轻子（来自电弱规范玻色子衰变）与非本征轻子（来自强子衰变或误识别），并在13.6 TeV质子-质子碰撞数据中完成开发、性能评估与数据驱动校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多轻子末态物理分析中，非本征轻子和误识别轻子构成关键背景，亟需更优的轻子隔离与本征性判别方法以提升信噪比。

Method: PLIT采用Transformer神经网络，融合轻子运动学变量与关联带电径迹的空间-动量信息，建模径迹间高阶相关性以增强prompt/non-prompt区分能力；使用tt̄模拟样本评估性能，并通过Z→ll事件对Run 3数据进行效率校准。

Result: PLIT相比传统隔离算法，在保持对非本征轻子高抑制率的同时，显著提升了本征轻子识别效率；已为电子与缪子定义操作点，并获得数据-模拟修正因子。

Conclusion: PLIT是一种鲁棒、可校准的深度学习型轻子鉴别器，已成功部署于ATLAS Run 3多轻子分析，为稀有过程搜寻与精确电弱测量提供关键技术支撑。

Abstract: The Prompt Lepton Isolation Tagger (PLIT) is a transformer-based algorithm designed to distinguish prompt leptons (electrons and muons) produced in the primary decays of electroweak gauge bosons from non-prompt leptons produced in hadron decays and from misidentified leptons. It is primarily used in measurements targeting multilepton final states with important backgrounds from non-prompt and misidentified leptons. PLIT exploits information from charged-particle tracks associated with the lepton candidate, together with lepton properties, using a transformer neural network to identify correlations between the tracks and improve the discrimination. Its performance is evaluated using simulated top-quark pair production events and compared with that of previously used lepton isolation algorithms, demonstrating improved prompt lepton efficiency while maintaining high rejection of non-prompt leptons. This article presents the development, performance, and calibration of PLIT for electrons and muons in proton--proton collisions at a centre-of-mass energy of $\sqrt{s}$=13.6 TeV recorded by the ATLAS experiment at the Large Hadron Collider. Operating points are defined for both electrons and muons, and their efficiencies are calibrated using $Z\to l^+l^-$ events in Run 3 data, providing data-to-simulation correction factors for physics analyses.

</details>


### [110] [Search for Higgs boson production in association with another Higgs or an exotic scalar boson in the $b\bar{b}W^{+}W^{-}$ single lepton final state at $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV with the ATLAS experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08241)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用ATLAS探测器在13 TeV质心能量下采集的140 fb⁻¹ Run 2数据，开展非共振与共振双Higgs产生过程的联合搜索：其中一Higgs衰变至bb̄，另一衰变至WW*→ℓνqq̄；未观测到显著信号，对标准模型双Higgs产率给出95% CL上限为35×σ_SM；同时对重标量X→SH（S→WW⁻, H→bb̄）共振过程在m_X∈[350,3000] GeV、m_S∈[170,2500] GeV参数空间设定了截面限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验标准模型中Higgs自耦合强度λ_HHH，并探查超出标准模型（BSM）的新物理信号，如级联衰变重标量X→SH，其可增强双Higgs末态并提供新质量尺度和耦合结构的敏感探针。

Method: 基于全Run 2数据（140 fb⁻¹），采用多轻子+强子型WW*衰变（ℓνqq̄）与bb̄标记联合重建，应用高级b-tagging、大横动量W喷注重建、以及针对非共振连续谱背景（tt̄、Z+jets、单Higgs等）的多变量判别分析（MVA）或形状拟合方法；对共振情形构造m_ℓνqq̄b̄b不变质量谱以提取X→SH信号。

Result: 非共振双Higgs产生无显著超出，观测上限σ(pp→HH) < 35 × σ_SM^(SM) @ 95% CL；对X→SH共振过程，在m_X–m_S平面上对不同质量组合给出了dσ/dm_X × BR(X→SH) × BR(S→WW) × BR(H→bb)的95% CL截面上限。

Conclusion: 当前精度下尚无法确认标准模型Higgs自耦合，但结果显著压缩了BSM参数空间（尤其是宽/窄宽度重标量模型），为未来HL-LHC及高亮度升级提供了关键基准与优化方向。

Abstract: A search for Higgs boson pair production where one Higgs boson decays via $H \to b \bar{b}$ and the other Higgs boson decays via $H \to WW^*\to \ell \nu q \bar{q}\quad (\ell=e,\mu)$ is presented. This search uses the complete Run 2 data sample recorded by the ATLAS detector at the LHC from 2015 to 2018 at a centre-of-mass energy of 13 TeV, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of $140$ fb$^{-1}$. The analysis looks for non-resonant Higgs boson pair production assuming Standard Model dynamics. No evidence for Higgs-boson pair production is observed and an upper limit at 95% confidence level is set at 35 times the Standard Model prediction. The same selection is used to target Beyond the Standard Model theories predicting the resonant production of the heavy scalars, $X$ and $S$, with $m_X > m_S > m_H$, undergoing the process $X \to SH, S \to W^+W^-, H \to b \bar{b}$. Limits are set on the resonant $X \to SH$ production for several combinations of the two scalar's masses within the $m_X$ mass range $350-3000$ GeV and $m_S$ mass range $170-2500$ GeV.

</details>
